# 1155 new apps — 2026-11-10

**Anomaly:** 1155 new apps is 292% of the median of the last 6 reports (396). Check for duplicates or a change in what Apple returns.

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-09 19:19 UTC (20:19 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-10 18:53 UTC (19:53 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-08 19:19 UTC (20:19 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.
